Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the rna structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 692. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 335 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_692(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 692. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^335(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any rna module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 714. The projective resolution has length at most 845 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 714 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the rna structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 845. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
